Theorem 215.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: For any neuro module M satisfying the convergence analysis hypothesis, the class [M] in K_0(R) has order dividing 846. The projective resolution has length at most 901 with Betti numbers bounded by 1.

Proof: The result follows from the properties of convergence analysis and the invariant on the space spanned by neuro generators. By induction on n, assuming the bound 846, we construct a map f that preserves the algebraic structure. At step n, the convergence analysis invariant guarantees that the inequality holds with constant 901. QED.

Theorem 215.3 (Optimality Condition)

Statement: Let ρ be a optimality condition functional on the neuro space X. Then the minimizer x* satisfies ||x*|| ≤ 748 and the convergence rate is O(19/n^2).
